Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) are increasingly used as general-purpose backbones, yet existing benchmarks cannot systematically diagnose their whole-slide cellular representation capabilities, including the decodability of cell-type information and the transferability of such information across tissue sections, datasets, and anatomical organs. We introduce CellPath-Bench, a cellular-resolution benchmark that evaluates frozen PFMs themselves. Following quality control of 52 candidate Xenium datasets, we construct a panel of 25 spatially aligned H\&E--Xenium tissue sections spanning 11 organs and 7,079,283 cells, harmonized into fine- and coarse-grained taxonomies. CellPath-Bench samples frozen WSI feature maps at registered nuclear coordinates and evaluates them using standardized multiclass linear probes. Cell Representation Advantage (CRA) measures the within-section advantage of nucleus-anchored representations over patch-level mean pooling, while Cell Representation Transferability (CRT) characterizes the generalization of cell-type decodability across tissue sections, datasets, and organs. We benchmark 30 pathology-specific and general-purpose foundation models through 304,920 runs across spatial readouts, magnifications, taxonomic granularities, and evaluation protocols. The results reveal substantial model-dependent differences in cell-type decodability and its cross-domain generalization, yielding distinct multidimensional capability profiles. CellPath-Bench provides a standardized framework for auditing cellular information in frozen PFM representations.

</details>


### [72] [Can Legal AI Know When It Is Wrong? And Do Students Know When It Is?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21089)
*Angel Mary John,Vipin Kumar Singh,Jerrin Thomas Panachakel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对印度司法系统中集成大型语言模型（LLM）进行了社会技术审计，识别出"自信惰性"现象——LLM在法律裁决中以接近最大置信度输出错误结果（类似于邓宁-克鲁格效应），并引入"高置信错误率"（HCER）量化这一风险。测试发现Meta AI最易受影响（31.7% HCER），同时通过N=380的法律学生调查揭示了人类对机器幻觉的脆弱性，提出对抗性法律研究教学法和源验证架构作为对策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM集成到印度司法系统虽有望提升司法可及性，但引入了严重风险。现有研究缺乏对LLM法律输出中"过度自信错误"的系统量化，也未充分探讨人类用户对这类错误的脆弱性，亟需一项社会技术层面的全面审计。

Method: 采用两阶段社会技术审计设计。第一阶段在印度1872年《合同法》及特定履行法定强制执行转变的背景下，对ChatGPT（GPT-5.2）、Meta AI和Perplexity AI进行60个案例的测试，引入高置信错误率（HCER）作为量化指标来度量置信度≥9/10时的错误判决率。第二阶段通过N=380的印度法律学生调查，考察人类对机器过自信输出的脆弱性，涉及捏造引文遭遇与验证行为的关系。

Result: 所有模型在法定更新问题上均表现不佳。Meta AI最脆弱（HCER 31.7%，平均置信度9.1/10），频繁误用修订前规则；Perplexity HCER为15.0%；ChatGPT HCER为6.7%。第二阶段显示，遭遇捏造引文的学生验证评分更高（4.2/5对比2.8/5），表明验证常作为对机器幻觉的反应性适应；81.6%的学生知晓提交幻影案例可构成藐视法庭，但71.1%未接受过AI伦理使用的正式培训。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM在法律领域"自信惰性"的系统性风险及其对人类用户的渗透效应。作者主张转向对抗性法律研究教学法，并实施基于源验证的架构，以防止系统性职业过失。强调了技术部署与法律教育层面双重干预的必要性。

Abstract: Integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) into the Indian judiciary promises access to justice but introduces severe risks. We identify the 'inertia of confidence'--an overconfidence phenomenon analogous to the Dunning-Kruger effect where LLMs provide incorrect legal verdicts with near-maximum confidence, driven by a hypothesized 'precedent overfitting' bias. Phase I of our socio-technical audit tested ChatGPT (GPT-5.2), Meta AI, and Perplexity AI on a 60-case battery regarding the Indian Contract Act, 1872, and the shift toward statutory enforcement of specific performance. We introduce the High-Confidence Error Rate (HCER) to quantify incorrect verdicts delivered with dangerous certainty (>= 9 on a 1-10 scale). All models struggled with statutory updates. Meta AI proved most vulnerable (31.7% HCER), frequently misapplying pre-amendment rules with a 9.1/10 mean confidence, followed by Perplexity (15.0%) and ChatGPT (6.7%).
  Phase II investigated human vulnerability to this overconfidence via a survey of Indian law students (N=380). Verification often functions as a reactive adaptation to machine hallucinations: students encountering fabricated citations reported higher verification scores (4.2/5) than those with no such encounters (2.8/5). Furthermore, while 81.6% knew submitting hallucinated cases can lead to contempt-of-court, 71.1% received no formal training on ethical AI use. We propose shifting toward adversarial legal research pedagogy and implementing source-grounded verification architectures to prevent systemic professional negligence.

</details>


### [73] [When Trust Meets Truth: Trust-Truth Separability in LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21097)
*Xin Sun,Di Wu,Yuchen Guo,Jiahuan Pei,Isao Echizen,Abdallah El Ali,Saku Sugawara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验了LLM-as-Judge系统中信任评分与真伪分类是否作为独立证据的假设。通过正确性受控的QA任务和仅改变来源线索的压力测试，发现LLM法官的信任评分与真伪判断高度耦合，且来源归因变化会同时影响信任分数、真伪判定及logit概率，表明信任评分不应被视为真伪判断的独立证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-Judge系统常输出多维评估（如可信度、可靠性、事实性），这些输出通常被当作相互独立的证据使用。作者希望验证这一基本假设是否成立，特别聚焦于信任评分与二元真伪分类这一常见判断对。

Method: 在正确性受控的QA数据集上，对比LLM法官的信任评分与真伪判定之间的对齐程度与人类行为参照的差异；随后设计压力测试，仅改变相同QA内容中的来源提示（Source cues，即标注为Human或AI生成），观察信任分数、真伪判定以及基于logit的正确侧概率如何随之变化。

Result: LLM法官在信任评分与真伪判定之间的对齐程度高于人类行为参照，表明两者的分离度较弱。在仅改变来源标注的压力测试中，来源归因的变化不仅影响了信任评分，也显著改变了真伪判定结果和logit层面的正确侧概率。

Conclusion: 当前LLM-as-Judge评估协议不应将信任评分视为真伪判断的独立证据——信任与真伪判断之间存在强耦合，且容易受来源归因等外部线索的干扰。

Abstract: LLM-as-Judge systems can produce multi-dimensional evaluations, such as trustworthiness, reliability, and factuality, and these outputs are often interpreted as independent evidence. We test this assumption for a common pair of judgments: trust scoring and binary truth classification. On correctness-controlled QA, LLM judges align trust scores with truth verdicts more tightly than human behavioral reference, suggesting weaker separations between trust and truth judgment. We then apply stress tests by changing only source cues of identical QA between Human and AI. Source attribution shifts not only trust scores but also truth verdicts and logit-derived correct-side probabilities. Results show that current LLM-as-Judge protocols should not treat trust scores as independent evidence for truth judgments.

</details>


### [74] [ReFrame: Evidence-Guided Test-Time Safety Alignment in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21100)
*Wenzheng Jiang,Xuankun Rong,Yuanzhao Zhai,Dawei Feng,Huaimin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReFrame，一个免训练的测试时多模态安全对齐框架。通过双代理机制（证据生成代理和重写路由代理）共享轻量级本地MLLM，在不修改或访问下游闭源MLLM内部信息的前提下，重构输入提示和图像路由，同时提升越狱防御、安全意识和降低过度敏感拒绝，并保持多模态效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态安全对齐方法依赖重新训练或内部状态检查，不适用于已部署的闭源MLLM。作者通过分析发现测试时安全对齐面临两个关键障碍：效用优势（utility dominance）导致模型忽视潜在风险，以及推理惯性（reasoning inertia）使模型沿恶意推理轨迹执行。因此需要一种不依赖模型内部信息的测试时安全对齐方法。

Method: 提出ReFrame框架，采用两代理协作架构，两者共享一个轻量级本地部署的MLLM：(1) 证据生成代理（evidence-generation agent）构建互补的风险证据和效用证据；(2) 重写和路由代理（rewrite-and-routing agent）将证据转化为安全的代理提示（proxy prompt）并做出图像路由决策，然后才调用下游闭源MLLM，全程不修改下游模型或访问其内部信息。

Result: 在多个MLLM和基准测试上的实验表明，ReFrame在提升越狱防御能力、增强安全意识和减少过度敏感拒绝方面均有改善，同时保持多模态效用不下降。

Conclusion: ReFrame验证了无需重新训练或内部状态访问的测试时多模态安全对齐的可行性，通过输入重构策略有效缓解了多模态安全对齐中的跨模态越狱、安全意识缺失和过度拒绝三大挑战。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend model capabilities beyond text, they also make safety alignment increasingly challenging. Multimodal safety alignment methods must address cross-modal jailbreaks, safety-awareness failures, and over-sensitive refusals. However, existing methods often rely on retraining or internal-state inspection, limiting their applicability to deployed closed-source MLLMs and motivating test-time safety alignment. We analyze this setting and identify two key obstacles, utility dominance and reasoning inertia, which cause models to overlook latent risks or follow malicious reasoning trajectories. Guided by these insights, we propose ReFrame, a training-free multimodal input reframing framework where two agents share a lightweight locally deployed MLLM: the evidence-generation agent constructs complementary risk and utility evidence, and the rewrite-and-routing agent converts it into a safe proxy prompt and image-routing decision before calling the downstream MLLM, without modifying it or accessing its internal information. Experiments across multiple MLLMs and benchmarks show that ReFrame improves jailbreak defense, safety awareness, and oversensitivity reduction while preserving multimodal utility.

</details>


### [75] [Large Language Models at the Intersection of Software Engineering and Software Security:An Evidence-Centered Structured Survey and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21107)
*Wei Lin,Tao Zhou,Zhaofei Xie,Changgui Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一项针对仓库级LLM智能体的结构化综述，综合了截至2026年5月31日的软件工程与软件安全评估工作，提出了一个涵盖功能正确性、安全性、运行可靠性、证据溯源和智能体权限的保障框架，并指出了现有评估中的效度威胁，进而提出最小化报告协议和研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从代码补全演变为仓库级智能体，能够检索上下文、编辑文件、执行工具并参与安全敏感工作流。然而现有证据体系在软件工程评估（以功能任务完成度为中心）与软件安全评估（以漏洞检测、安全生成或利用验证为中心）之间呈现割裂状态，缺乏统一框架来综合评判这些系统的能力。

Method: 作者采用证据导向的结构化综述方法，综合了截至2026年5月31日的代表性工作，从软件工程任务、软件安全任务、适应机制、工件粒度、评估设计五个维度进行系统梳理。在任务分类法之外，引入了将功能正确性、安全性、运行可靠性、证据溯源和智能体权限分离的保障框架，并推导出最小化报告协议以支持跨研究比较。

Result: 综述发现：(1) 执行反馈和仓库访问能显著提升工程任务完成度，但本身不足以建立安全性保证；(2) 静态分析标签或漏洞分类分数很少能确证可部署的正确性；(3) 识别出反复出现的效度威胁——弱测试预言机、重复和时间泄露数据、变化的智能体工装、仅代理的安全检查、预算和人工干预报告不足等。

Conclusion: 模型能力应被视为一个由任务适当证据支撑的保障论证（assurance case），而非由单一基准分数来评判。研究议程优先关注联合安全与功能的基准、仓库级威胁模型、校准的人工监督、纵向可维护性证据以及可复现的智能体评估。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are moving from code completion toward repository-scale agents that retrieve context, edit files, execute tools, and participate in security-sensitive workflows. The evidence for these systems, however, remains divided between software engineering evaluations centered on functional task completion and software security evaluations centered on vulnerability detection, secure generation, or exploit-oriented validation. This evidence-centered structured survey synthesizes representative work available through May 31, 2026 across software engineering tasks, software security tasks, adaptation mechanisms, artifact granularity, and evaluation design. In addition to a task taxonomy, we introduce an assurance framework that separates functional correctness, security, operational reliability, evidence provenance, and agent authority. The review shows that execution feedback and repository access can substantially improve engineering task completion, but do not by themselves establish security; conversely, static-analysis labels or vulnerability-classification scores rarely establish deployable correctness. We identify recurring validity threats--weak test oracles, duplicated and temporally leaked data, changing agent harnesses, proxy-only security checks, and under-reported budgets and human intervention--and derive a minimum reporting protocol for cross-study comparison. The resulting research agenda prioritizes jointly secure-and-functional benchmarks, repository-scale threat models, calibrated human oversight, longitudinal maintainability evidence, and reproducible agent evaluation. The central conclusion is that model capability should be judged as an assurance case supported by task-appropriate evidence, rather than by a single benchmark score.

</details>


### [76] [Root cause analysis via difference graph discovery from linear time-series data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21117)
*Anouk Ruer,Timothée Loranchet,Daria Bystrova,Charles K. Assaad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究线性时间序列中的根因分析，将其视为差异图发现问题，聚焦于因果系数在正常与异常状态间发生变化的"违背效应"根因变量，通过改造面向两个群体的差异图发现方法至时间序列设置，并在模拟数据和真实数据（IT监控、重症监护）上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂动态系统中的根因分析旨在识别导致异常的机制。现有工作主要面向两个群体间的差异图发现，但尚未系统地将这一视角推广到时间序列场景，特别是针对因果系数在正常与异常状态间变化的根因定位问题。

Method: 使用线性离散时间动态结构因果模型对问题进行形式化建模，将原本用于两个群体间差异图发现的多种方法适配到时间序列设置中，用正常状态和异常状态替代原有的两个群体，从而发现这两者之间的差异因果图。

Result: 在模拟数据上评估了所提出的多种方法，并在IT监控与重症监护监控的真实数据集上验证了其实用价值，结果表明差异图发现能够有效定位导致异常行为的因果机制。

Conclusion: 差异图发现为时间序列中的根因分析提供了一种可行的形式化框架，能够帮助定位由因果系数变化（效应违背型根因）导致的异常，在IT监控和医疗监控等领域具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Root cause analysis aims to identify the mechanisms responsible for anomalies in complex dynamical systems. In this paper, we study root cause analysis in linear time-series through the lens of difference graph discovery. We focus on effect-defying root causes, corresponding to variables whose causal coefficients change between a normal and an anomalous regime. We formalize this problem using linear discrete-time dynamic structural causal models and adapt several methods originally introduced for discovering difference graphs between two populations to the time-series setting, where the two populations are replaced by a normal and an anomalous regime. We first evaluate the proposed approaches on simulated data, and then demonstrate their practical relevance on real-world datasets from IT monitoring and intensive care monitoring. Our results show how difference graph discovery can help localize causal mechanisms responsible for anomalous behavior.

</details>


### [77] [From Attention Masks to Inert Zero-Vector Tokens: OAttention and O-Closure for Token Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21174)
*Heyang Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OAttention：一种基于token活性系数p_i=||h_i||²/(τ+||h_i||²)的注意力机制，将token表示为"支持耦合"的零元素，使得零向量token成为注意力中的零元素，从而精确支持空接收者、空源插入、自注意力插入和空支持等性质，并由此构建OTransformer架构。通过契约测试和GPU评估验证，在TabPFN v3回归器零微调改造中RMSE变化极小，但消融实验表明仅OAttention不足以保证NULL状态传递，须依赖完整OTransformer路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力掩码只控制查询-源对的交互关系，却无法提供一种由表示携带的、在注意力边界上不参与计算的状态（即token级的存在性/活跃性控制）。作者希望引入一种token级"活性"概念，使零向量token在代数意义上真正成为注意力运算的零元素，从而支持精确的NULL状态插入、删除和组合性质，为缺失值处理、动态结构建模等提供形式化基础。

Method: 为每个token的隐藏载体h_i定义活性系数p_i=||h_i||²/(τ+||h_i||²)。该系数双重角色：一是门控token i发出信息的强度，二是作为token i进入与其他token共享计算的质量权重。OAttention实现该规则：接收端输出由p_i门控，源端j分别以p_j加权注意力的分子和配分函数，同时保留标准score、可见性关系、指数竞争和值聚合。由此衍生出局部O组件（OFFN、ONorm、OInject）、presence加权的OStandardize、O-Closure定律M(H⊕0)=M(H)⊕0，并通过残差和组合闭包构建OTransformer。

Result: 通过契约测试验证算子的精确性；GPU评估中，对克隆的TabPFN v3回归器进行零微调改造，校准的隐藏载体OAttention和Full-O变体在18个数据集-种子配对中平均RMSE分别变化+0.088%和+0.177%。两模块消融表明，仅OAttention无法让NULL状态穿过普通宿主组件，而OTransformer路径可以。作者明确限定这些是作用域测试，不宣称通用无损性、任意宿主闭包、学习的原点吸引或缺失值的一般语义。

Conclusion: OAttention以token级活性系数为核心，实现了一个在支持耦合意义下具有零元素性质的注意力算子，并通过O-Closure定律和组合闭包构建出能保持NULL状态的OTransformer。论文在精确性、活动路径兼容性和组合必要性方面给出了受范围限定的验证，但承认不涵盖通用无损失保证和缺失值的全面语义。

Abstract: Attention masks are relation-level controls: they specify which query--source pairs may interact. They do not provide a representation-carried token state that is non-participating at the attention boundary. We assign each token hidden carrier \(h_i\) an active-presence coefficient \(p_i=\lVert h_i\rVert^2/(τ+\lVert h_i\rVert^2)\). The same coefficient has two roles: it gates information emitted by token \(i\), and it determines the mass with which token \(i\) enters computations shared with other tokens.
  OAttention is the support-coupled attention realization of this rule. It gates the receiver output by \(p_i\) and weights source \(j\) by \(p_j\) in both the attention numerator and partition, while retaining the standard score, visibility relation, exponential competition, and value aggregation. This makes the zero-vector token a zero element and yields exact null-receiver, null-source insertion, self-attention insertion, and empty-support properties. The same token-level presence gives local O-components (OFFN, ONorm, and OInject), presence-weighted OStandardize, the O-Closure law \(M(H\oplus0)=M(H)\oplus0\), and an OTransformer by residual and compositional closure.
  The canonical operator is checked by contract tests and a GPU evaluation. In a zero-fine-tuning retrofit of a cloned pretrained TabPFN v3 regressor, calibrated hidden-carrier OAttention and Full-O variants change mean RMSE by $+0.088\%$ and $+0.177\%$, respectively, over 18 matched dataset--seed cases. A two-block ablation shows that OAttention alone does not preserve a NULL state through ordinary host components, whereas the OTransformer path does. These are scoped tests of exactness, active-path compatibility, and compositional necessity; they do not establish universal no-loss, arbitrary-host closure, learned attraction to the origin, or a general semantics for missing values.

</details>


### [78] [SENTRY: Deterministic, Intelligent Risk Assessment for IT Change Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21203)
*Daniel Arulpragasam,Christer Henrysson,Ella Ly,Deepika Anbalagan,Leo Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SENTRY是一个用于大型金融机构技术变更管理的风险评估平台，用确定性机器学习管道（XGBoost + 混合RAG）替代主观问卷评分，实现ROC AUC 0.87和85%准确率，高风险变更检出率约为现有流程的3.25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型金融机构的技术变更管理依赖风险评估，但现有自报式问卷主观、易被操纵，且难以区分常规变更与后续引发重大事故的变更，需要更准确、一致、可审计的评估方法。

Method: SENTRY采用确定性机器学习管道：以梯度提升决策树（XGBoost）为核心模型，结合混合检索增强生成（RAG），融合结构化运维元数据、应用依赖图、历史事故记录，通过语义+词汇混合搜索历史变更请求，将非结构化文本中的风险信号压缩为单一标量特征后输入模型，并通过SHAP值保持逐预测可解释性。

Result: 在企业级规模变更数据上，SENTRY达到ROC AUC 0.87和85%整体准确率，高风险变更检测率约为现有流程的3.25倍。

Conclusion: 论文最后审视了这一设计的架构权衡及其对受监管变更管理中应用机器学习的启示，说明在保持确定性和可解释性的前提下，机器学习可以显著优于问卷式风险评估。

Abstract: Technology change management in large financial institutions depends on risk assessments that are accurate, consistent, and auditable. In practice, many institutions still rely on self-reported questionnaires. Those questionnaires are subjective, easy to game, and poor at separating routine changes from the ones that later trigger major incidents. This paper presents SENTRY, a risk assessment platform that replaces questionnaire-based scoring with a deterministic machine learning pipeline built from gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost) and hybrid retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The system combines structured operational metadata, application dependency graphs, and historical incident records with a hybrid semantic and lexical search over historical change requests. The retrieval step captures the risk signal in unstructured change request text, then compresses that signal into a single scalar feature before model inference. That design keeps the model deterministic and preserves per-prediction explainability via SHAP values. Evaluated on enterprise-scale change data, SENTRY achieves a ROC AUC of 0.87 and 85% overall accuracy, and it detects high-risk changes at roughly 3.25 times the rate of the existing process. We close by examining the architectural trade-offs behind this design and what they imply for the use of machine learning in regulated change management.

</details>


### [79] [Personalized Privacy Control in LLMs via Attention Head Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21209)
*Junseok Kim,Nakyeong Yang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"个性化隐私"（Personalized Privacy）概念，将用户特定的披露偏好纳入LLM隐私控制，并构建了P3Bench基准（包含个性化披露策略的上下文隐私策略扩展）及Repair方法（推理时注意力头干预），以改善模型遵循用户特定隐私偏好的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文隐私研究仅关注LLM是否依据上下文规范来调节信息披露，但忽略了即使在相同上下文中，不同用户的可接受披露边界也可能不同。这一局限性阻碍了代理式AI（agentic AI）在访问多样化用户数据时实现真正个性化的隐私保护。

Method: 1) 提出"个性化隐私"概念，将用户特定的披露偏好整合进隐私控制；2) 构建P3Bench基准，将上下文隐私策略扩展为包含个性化披露策略；3) 提出Repair方法，一种推理时的注意力头干预方法，通过调整注意力头的注意力模式来改变模型的披露行为，使其朝向符合策略的响应。

Result: 实验表明，基于提示的策略无法可靠地执行个性化隐私策略，Qwen2.5-7B和Gemma3-4B的平均策略忽略率分别高达51.25%和74.28%。Repair方法显著提高了模型对用户特定隐私偏好的遵循程度，减少了模型未能遵循给定策略的情况。

Conclusion: 个性化隐私是上下文隐私的重要补充，提示式策略在个性化隐私约束下不可靠，而推理时注意力头干预（Repair）是一种有效的改进途径，可显著提升LLM对个性化隐私策略的遵循能力。

Abstract: The rise of agentic AI enables LLMs to access diverse user data, raising critical privacy concerns. Prior work on contextual privacy studies whether LLMs regulate information disclosure according to context-dependent norms. However, acceptable disclosure boundaries may vary across users even within the same context. To address this limitation, we introduce \textit{personalized privacy}, which incorporates user-specific disclosure preferences into privacy control. We further present P3Bench~(\textbf{P}ersonalized \textbf{P}rivacy \textbf{P}reservation \textbf{Bench}mark), a novel benchmark extending contextual privacy policies with personalized disclosure policies. Experiments show that prompt-based policies fail to reliably enforce personalized privacy policies, with Qwen2.5-7B and Gemma3-4B showing average policy ignorance ratios of 51.25\% and 74.28\%, respectively. Finally, to address this problem, we propose \textsc{Repair}, a robust inference-time attention head intervention method that adjusts disclosure behavior toward policy-consistent responses. Our method significantly improves adherence to user-specific privacy preferences by reducing cases where the model fails to follow the given policy.

</details>


### [80] [Enhancing LLMs in Predictive Political QA with Semi-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21218)
*Yinan Liu,Zihan Zhou,Zichun Jin,Xinyu Wang,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PSL框架，一个用于预测性政治问答的双视角框架，通过提取演员立场信号（语义视角）和从演员交互图中学习结构感知表示（向量视角），将半结构化政治记录转化为面向推理的证据，在多个数据集和LLM上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性政治问答（如预测政治行为体如何投票）超越了事实查询范畴。现有的LLM增强方法（如基于演员档案模拟和知识图谱证据注入）虽然提升了政治推理能力，但大多将外部资源视为基于知识的证据，忽略了预测相关的信号（如演员立场和高阶依赖关系），导致预测性能受限。

Method: 提出PSL（双视角框架）：1）语义视角——从与问题相关的演员记录中提取立场信号（stance signals），捕捉问题特定偏好；2）向量视角——从演员交互图（actor interaction graph）学习结构感知的演员表示，捕捉政治行为体之间的间接依赖（高阶结构信号）。两种视角互补地将半结构化政治记录转化为面向LLMs的推理证据。

Result: 在三个真实数据集和多个LLM上，PSL一致性地优于基线方法。消融实验证实立场信号和结构信号的互补增益。

Conclusion: 预测性政治QA需要超越知识型证据，显式建模立场与高阶结构两类互补的预测信号；PSL通过双视角设计有效融合这两类信号，为预测性政治问答提供了更优的LLM增强方案。

Abstract: Predictive political question answering (QA), such as predicting how a political actor will vote, goes beyond factual lookup. External political resources offer rich historical evidence, but rarely contain the answer itself. Existing LLM augmentation methods, including actor-profile-based simulation and knowledge graph evidence injection, improve political reasoning but largely treat external resources as knowledge-based evidence, leaving prediction-relevant signals under-modeled. We identify two complementary signals for predictive political QA: actor stances that capture issue-specific preferences, and high-order structure signals that capture indirect dependencies among political actors. We propose PSL, a dual-view framework that converts semi-structured political records into inference-oriented evidence for LLMs. PSL extracts stance signals from question-relevant actor records in a semantic view, and learns structure-aware actor representations from an actor interaction graph in a vector view. Across three real-world datasets and multiple LLMs, PSL consistently outperforms baselines, with ablations confirming the complementary gains of stance and structure signals.

</details>


### [81] [Ontology-supported AI Model and Dataset Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21224)
*Jan Novacek,Ali Ahari,Tobias Müller,Sebastian Reiter,Alexander Viehl,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个AI模型交换平台，该平台整合了本体论（ontology）以促进AI模型和数据集的交换、使用与分析，旨在解决工业环境中AI资产管理与交换中的语义鸿沟问题，并通过一个实时关键系统用例展示其效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI研究与应用的发展，不同组织生成和使用大量需要跟踪、追溯和管理的AI资产（模型、数据集等），但现有方法缺乏有效的资产发现与语义对齐机制，导致工业环境中AI模型及相关资产的交换和管理存在语义鸿沟。作者旨在回答"如何无语义鸿沟地有效交换和管理AI模型及相关资产"这一核心问题。

Method: 提出了一个AI模型交换平台，该平台的核心特征是整合了一个本体论（ontology），用于建立对AI模型与数据集交换、使用和分析所需概念的共同理解，从而弥合语义鸿沟。平台支持AI模型和数据集的存储、交换、分析与发现。

Result: 通过在实时关键系统（real-time critical systems）场景中展示一个用例，验证了平台在促进AI模型和数据集交换、管理方面的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该平台结合本体论的方法能够有效支持工业环境中AI模型及相关资产的交换与管理，消除语义鸿沟，并为AI资产的追踪、发现和透明比较提供基础设施支撑。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a great deal of research into improving AI methods and their application. The main focus is on tracking progress, enabling transparent comparisons, and fostering a more profound understanding of AI. In that process, different organizations generate and use plenty of assets that need to be tracked, traced and managed. Moreover, it is important to discover assets relevant for the task at hand. This paper presents research aiming to contribute to answering the question of what is required to exchange and manage AI models and related assets effectively without semantic gaps in an industrial context. We introduce a platform for AI model exchange, which facilitates the usage, exchange, and analysis of AI models and datasets. The platform incorporates an ontology that can foster a more profound common understanding of what is required in these tasks and help tackle the issues mentioned above. Finally, we elucidate the utility of the platform through the illustration of a use case in the context of real-time critical systems.

</details>


### [82] [Fine-Grain GPU Parallelization of the Generalized Partition Crossover for Large-Scale Traveling Salesman Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21233)
*Swetha Varadarajan,Darrell Whitley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大规模TSP问题的广义分区交叉（GPX）算子分区阶段的细粒度GPU实现，通过图并行重构、合并内存布局、幽灵节点变换和连通分量分析，在CUDA上并行化父代环联合、四度顶点分割、公共边删除和重组分量识别。在1万到2百万城市的实例上实现了48倍到625倍的速度提升，并显著降低内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于遗传算法（GA）的TSP求解器（如EAX）在基准测试上表现优异，但在大规模并行架构上的可扩展性受限，因为交叉操作涉及不规则内存访问、图遍历和顺序依赖。现有GPU TSP求解器主要利用群体级并行且仅适用于较小规模问题，需要一种细粒度的算子级并行方案来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 将GPX分区阶段重构为图并行问题，采用合并内存布局、幽灵节点转换和连通分量分析等技术。在CUDA上并行实现四个关键步骤：父代环的并集构建、四度顶点的分裂、公共边的删除以及重组分量的识别。通过算子级（而非群体级）并行在GPU上实现细粒度加速。

Result: 在包含1万到2百万个城市的TSP实例上，与朴素顺序CPU实现相比，GPU分区实现了48倍至625倍的加速比，同时显著减少了内存开销。

Conclusion: 研究表明算子级并行能够显著提升基于GA的TSP求解器在现代众核架构上的可扩展性，为大规模TSP问题的高效求解提供了新的并行化路径。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is one of the most extensively studied NP-hard optimization problems. Genetic Algorithm (GA)-based solvers, such as the Edge Assembly Crossover (EAX), achieve state-of-the-art performance on many benchmark instances. However, the scalability of these approaches in massively parallel architectures remains limited because crossover operations involve irregular memory access patterns, graph traversals, and sequential dependencies. Existing GPU-based TSP solvers primarily exploit population-level parallelism and are limited to relatively small problem sizes. This work presents a fine-grain GPU implementation of the partition phase of the Generalized Partition Crossover (GPX) operator for large-scale TSP instances. The proposed approach reformulates GPX partitioning as a graph-parallel problem using coalesced memory layouts, ghost-node transformations, and connected-component analysis. The im- plementation parallelizes the union of parent tours, the splitting of degree- four vertices, the deletion of common edges, and the identification of recombining components using CUDA. Experimental results on instances ranging from 10,000 to 2 million cities demonstrate substantial acceleration over a naive sequential CPU imple- mentation. The proposed GPU partitioning achieves speedups between 48x and 625x while significantly reducing memory overhead. The re- sults demonstrate that operator-level parallelism can substantially im- prove the scalability of GA-based TSP solvers on modern many-core architectures.

</details>


### [83] [CLEAR: Continuous Latent Adapter Routing for Utility-Preserving LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21278)
*Chengxiao Wang,Enyi Jiang,Xiaojing Liao,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLEAR（连续潜在适配器路由）是一种条件安全适配框架，通过轻量级隐藏状态门控持续控制安全低秩适配器的激活强度，在提升LLM安全性的同时最小化对良性输入的效用损失。在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上，CLEAR将HarmBench ASR从32.3%降至0.5%，同时比全局SFT或LoRA在GSM8K上高出7.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM安全对齐方法（如全局SFT或标准LoRA）通常以牺牲效用为代价，因为它们对有害和良性输入统一施加安全微调，导致模型在正常任务上性能下降。作者希望设计一种机制，能够在不显著影响良性提示性能的前提下提升模型的安全性，从而改善安全-效用的权衡。

Method: CLEAR框架包含三个核心组件：1）一个轻量级隐藏状态门控（hidden-state gate），用于动态判断当前输入是否需要安全干预；2）一个安全低秩适配器（safety LoRA adapter），仅在需要时被激活；3）通过连续控制适配器的激活强度（而非二元的开/关），实现更精细的安全-效用平衡。整个框架保持骨干模型（backbone）冻结，只通过门控机制调节安全适配器的贡献。

Result: 在HarmBench和常见效用基准上的实验表明：1）CLEAR将Llama-3-8B-Instruct的HarmBench ASR从32.3%降至0.5%；2）相比全局SFT或标准LoRA，CLEAR在GSM8K上保持高达7.1个百分点的准确率优势；3）CLEAR在提升鲁棒性的同时显著减少了效用退化。

Conclusion: CLEAR通过条件化、连续的安全适配机制有效改善了LLM对齐中的安全-效用权衡，是一种有前景的安全对齐方案，为未来在保持模型能力的同时增强安全性的研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) often comes at the expense of utility, as globally applied safety tuning may affect model responses to both harmful and benign inputs. We propose \textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoiding unnecessary changes to the frozen backbone that could degrade performance on benign prompts. Experiments on widely used safety and utility benchmarks show that CLEAR improves robustness on HarmBench while reducing the utility degradation observed with globally applied safety tuning such as SFT or standard low-rank adaptation (LoRA). On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR reduces HarmBench ASR from 32.3\% to 0.5\%, while retaining most of the base model's utility and achieving up to 7.1 percentage points higher GSM8K accuracy than globally applied SFT or LoRA. These results suggest that CLEAR is a promising mechanism for improving the safety--utility trade-off in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [84] [AUSO: Action-Level Unified Skill Optimization from Internalization to Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21292)
*Huizu Lin,Chengkai Huang,Tianqi Gao,Tao Huang,Daijiao Liu,Tongxin Li,Xiaoyan Sun,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AUSO（行动级统一技能优化）框架，在一个统一的强化学习框架下建模技能在智能体策略演化中的完整生命周期——从初期提供可学习知识，到中期支持能力形成，再到后期仅在提升个体决策时被调用。AUSO通过渐进式、行动感知的优化过程，将外部技能监督逐步内化为决策知识，并在多个基准任务上取得优于竞争基线的性能与泛化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能建模技能在策略演化中的生命周期。它们要么将技能保持在模型外部（未能内化），要么完全内化技能（丧失对技能使用的灵活控制），要么通过噪声较大的任务级成功率在内部化与利用目标之间手动切换。此外，这些设计将训练碎片化，且将同一轨迹内的所有行动分配同等重要性，忽略了技能指导可能对某些决策有帮助而对另一些决策造成干扰的事实。

Method: AUSO通过三种渐进式阶段实现统一：1）训练初期，联合从教师指导和环境结果学习，获取基础技能同时不丢失任务导向反馈；2）随后强调基于结果的策略优化，巩固自主问题解决能力；3）策略成熟后，在每个采样行动上同时评估技能条件化与无技能两种情境，生成行动级信息信号，并与轨迹结果优势相结合，使有益于技能敏感的行动获得更强的更新，有害行动被抑制。整个过程中强化学习作为贯穿所有阶段的共享骨干。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SearchQA三个基准任务上的实验表明，AUSO在智能体性能和分布外泛化能力上均一致优于竞争基线。

Conclusion: AUSO通过行动级的技能评估与渐进式优化，成功地将技能从外部监督源演变为其利用方式与行动级收益相适应的决策知识，解决了现有方法在技能生命周期建模上的碎片化与行动重要性均等化问题，验证了统一的强化学习框架能够同时支持技能学习与技能利用的全过程。

Abstract: Skills play different roles as an agent's policy evolves: they should first provide learnable knowledge, then support capability formation, and finally be invoked only when they improve individual decisions. Existing methods rarely model this lifecycle. They either keep skills outside the model, fully internalize them, or select among internalization and utilization objectives through noisy task-level success rates. Such designs fragment training and assign uniform importance to actions within the same trajectory, even though skill guidance may help some decisions while distracting others. To solve these problems, we introduce AUSO (Action-level Unified Skill Optimization), which unifies skill learning and skill use through a progressive, action-aware optimization process. At the beginning of training, AUSO jointly learns from teacher guidance and environmental outcomes, enabling the policy to acquire foundational skills without losing task-oriented feedback. It subsequently emphasizes outcome-based policy optimization to consolidate autonomous problem-solving ability. As the policy matures, AUSO evaluates each sampled action under both skill-conditioned and skill-free contexts. The resulting action-level information signal is coupled with the trajectory outcome advantage, allowing beneficial skill-sensitive actions to receive stronger updates and harmful ones to be suppressed. Therefore, skills gradually transition from an external source of supervision into decision knowledge whose utilization is adapted to its action-level benefit, while reinforcement learning remains the shared backbone across all stages. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA show that AUSO consistently improves agent performance and out-of-distribution generalization over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [85] [From Regulation to Implementation: A Critical Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Regulatory Compliance in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21317)
*Adriana Watson,Marco Bücheler,Grant Richards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在生成欧盟合规文档（如数字产品护照DPP和数据保护影响评估DPIA）时的质量问题，重点探讨数据提取指令和监管模糊性如何影响LLM输出的质量与一致性。实验发现：监管指南较模糊（如DPIA）时需要更高的上下文提示来维持一致性和完整性，而指南较严格（如数字电池护照DBP）时输出一致但在提示上下文不足时更易产生幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟陆续出台ESPR（引入数字产品护照）和GDPR（要求DPIA）等法规，但创建这些合规文档面临两大挑战：工业数据格式异构且分散于公司和供应商系统中难以提取；DPIA文档需要跨学科专业知识且无标准格式。虽有研究者提出用LLM辅助生成，但数据提取难度和法规模糊性对LLM输出的影响尚未被系统研究，这是本文要填补的研究空白。

Method: 本文系统性地比较了不同LLM模型在生成两种类型合规文档（DPP和DPIA）时的表现，通过设置不同的数据提取指令和不同的提示上下文（context prompts）来测试监管指南严格程度差异的影响，并以人工构建的ground-truth schema为基准，对生成的合规文档进行质量和一致性评估。

Result: 实验结果表明：对于监管指南较模糊的DPIA，需要更高上下文的提示才能维持输出的一致性和完整性；而对于监管指南较严格的数字电池护照（DBP）格式，提示上下文变化对结果一致性影响不大，但低上下文时更容易在输出中产生幻觉（hallucinations）。

Conclusion: LLM在辅助生成合规文档时，其表现受监管指南的明确程度显著影响。法规模糊性会放大LLM对提示上下文的依赖，而严格的格式约束虽能提高一致性却增加了幻觉风险。这提示在设计和部署LLM辅助合规工具时，需要根据具体法规的明确程度调整提示工程策略。

Abstract: The European Union (EU) has emerged as a leading regulatory body in the development of sustainability and privacy regulations. While new regulation requirements vary, many include a documentation artifact to ensure compliance. Notably, the Ecodesign for Sustainable Products Regulation (ESPR) introduces Digital Product Passports (DPPs) for life cycle transparency, while the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) mandates Data Protection Impact Assessments (DPIAs) to mitigate privacy risks. Creating these compliance artifacts, however, is challenging. Industrial data, which often exists in heterogeneous formats and is scattered across company and supplier systems, is required for DPPs and can be difficult to extract into compliant DPP formatting. Furthermore, DPIA documents require interdisciplinary expertise and follow no standardized format, making development difficult for novel systems. To address the particular complexity of compliance artifact creation for both regulations, researchers have proposed the use of LLMs in the generation process; however, the impact of the aforementioned problems on the output of these systems is largely unaddressed. This work investigates the existing research gap by exploring how data extraction instructions and regulatory vagueness impact the quality and consistency of LLM-produced compliance artifacts. The resulting artifacts are evaluated by benchmarking different models against manually created ground-truth schemas. The results reveal that less strict guidelines, such as DPIA formatting, require higher context prompts to maintain consistency and completeness. Stricter guidelines, such as formatting for Digital Battery Passports (DBP), result in consistent results regardless of prompt context, but may lead to more hallucinations in the output

</details>


### [86] [Unified Branch-and-Bound Search for the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem on Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21319)
*Jingtao Tang,Hang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了凸集图上的Steiner旅行商问题（Steiner-TSP on GCS），并提出了一种统一的基于分支定界的搜索框架，通过裁剪前缀开销与连通流松弛下界来求解，给出ε最优性保证，并在移动机械臂巡检任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在凸集图（GCS）上的旅行商问题中引入Steiner变体（允许可选中转顶点与重复访问）后，解空间变为无限维，现有方法难以统一处理路径顺序、连续轨迹与传感模式等联合决策，因此需要一种可证明有界且高效的求解框架。

Method: 提出统一的分支定界搜索，在根步行（rooted walk）前缀上展开。采用加性下界图成本约束承诺前缀，利用割分离的连通流松弛下界化访问所有剩余目标并返回根节点的残差成本。在均匀正成本假设下，证明广度优先遍历在无初始可行解时经有限次扩展终止，深度优先遍历在有有限初始可行解时亦然；对用户指定的因子ε≥1，全局下界可证明任一策略的当前最优解成本不超过全局最优解的ε倍。还引入了LTL_f表达的动作前序约束以联合决策传感模式、访问顺序与连续轨迹。

Result: 两种遍历策略在所有基准实例上30秒内均能找到可行解，平均认证最优性间隙分别为28.1%和29.7%；而两种近期基线方法仅在大约一半的实例上成功。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一分支定界框架能够有效求解Steiner-TSP on GCS，同时保证ε因子的次优性上界，并在移动机械臂巡检等实际任务中显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: We formalize the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem (Steiner-TSP) on Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS), which seeks a minimum-cost closed trajectory through required convex sets while allowing optional transit vertices and revisits. To explore the resulting infinite solution space, we propose a unified branch-and-bound search over rooted walk prefixes. Additive lower-bound-graph costs bound committed prefixes, while a cut-separated connected-flow relaxation lower-bounds the residual cost of visiting every remaining target and returning to the root. Under a uniform positive-cost assumption, best-first traversal terminates after finitely many expansions on every feasible instance without an initial incumbent, whereas depth-first traversal does so once a finite incumbent is available. For a user-specified factor $ε\geq1$, a global lower bound certifies that either strategy's incumbent cost is at most $ε$ times the global optimum. We further demonstrate joint sensing-mode, visitation-order, and continuous-trajectory selection for a mobile-manipulator inspection task, including action precedences expressed in linear temporal logic over finite traces (LTL$_f$). Both traversal strategies find feasible solutions on all benchmark instances within 30s with mean certified optimality gaps of 28.1% and 29.7%, respectively, whereas two recent baselines succeed on only about half of the instances

</details>


### [87] [Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks: Encoding Anatomic Priors in Loss and Architecture, with an SE(3) Formulation of Guidewire-Induced Aortoiliac Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21332)
*David P. Stonko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出解剖感知神经网络(AINN)框架，将软/硬解剖先验分别以损失惩罚项和架构约束方式嵌入深度学习模型，以解决解剖学合理性与数据稀疏泛化问题。在主动脉-髂动脉树受腔内导线变形这一临床案例上，将血管中心线和导线路径提升到SE(3)李群的曲线(frame)空间，通过Cosserat杆-血管耦合与单侧腔道接触不等式建模，以受约束弹性极小化器作为预测，采用Wasserstein-2最优运输损失实现2D血管造影训练3D预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解剖学深度学习模型可能数值上合理但解剖学上不可能，且在数据稀缺时泛化能力差。作者希望借鉴物理信息神经网络(PINN)的思路，将解剖学先验嵌入网络，使预测既在解剖学上可行，又在数据有限时保持良好的泛化性能，并针对现代主动脉外科和自主腔内导航这一实际临床需求进行验证。

Method: AINN框架包含两类先验：(1) 软解剖先验作为损失中的惩罚项（如肾移植动脉从髂动脉而非主动脉发出的分支惩罚）；(2) 硬解剖先验构建在架构与状态表示中（如血管连续性），使无效预测在构造上不可能。具体实现：将血管中心线和导线路径从R^3提升至SE(3)李群的frame曲线；Cosserat杆导线与扭转调制的解剖锚定血管通过单侧腔道接触不等式耦合；预测为受约束弹性能量的极小化器，接触力作为Lagrange乘子；训练损失为2D血管造影C臂投影与预测投影之间的Wasserstein-2最优运输损失。

Result: 运动学、损失函数和投影与已知真值验证一致；力学求解器仅针对自身最优化条件验证，预测位移尚未达到网格收敛，且当前未训练任何网络。该结果展示了在数据有限场景下将解剖强制约束嵌入求解流程的可行性，可验证的2D到3D监督管线已建立。

Conclusion: AINN为深度学习解剖建模提供了结合软、硬解剖先验的系统框架，在有限数据下可产生解剖学上不可能的预测被构造性地排除。未来工作将把该in silico模型迁移到真实CT扫描数据，检验是否提升预测精度并降低训练数据需求。

Abstract: Deep-learning models of anatomy can be numerically plausible yet anatomically impossible, and they generalize poorly when data are scarce. We introduce Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks (AINN), in which soft anatomic priors enter as penalty terms in the loss (e.g., a branching penalty that treats a renal transplant artery off the iliac instead of the aorta as unexpected rather than impossible), in direct analogy to a physics-informed neural network, and hard anatomic priors (e.g., continuity of the vessel) are built into the architecture and state representation, making such invalid predictions impossible by construction wherever the prior admits architectural enforcement.
  We develop it on a clinical test case with limited data: how the aortoiliac tree deforms when a stiff wire is introduced endoluminally. This is important to contemporary aortic surgery and will matter to autonomous endovascular navigation. We lift the vessel centerline and the wire path from R^3 to curves of frames in the Lie group SE(3), and couple a Cosserat-rod wire to a tortuosity-modulated, anatomically anchored vessel through a unilateral lumen-contact inequality. The prediction is a constrained minimizer of the coupled elastic energy, with contact forces as its Lagrange multipliers. Supervision is a Wasserstein-2 optimal-transport loss between the predicted projection through the C-arm geometry and the observed angiogram, so a 2D angiogram can train a 3D prediction. The kinematics, loss and projection are verified against known ground truth; the mechanics solver only against its own optimality conditions, and predicted displacement is not yet mesh-converged. Here, no network is trained. Future work will transfer this in silico model to real CT scans and test whether it improves predictive accuracy and reduces the training data required.

</details>


### [88] [VIALS: A Benchmark for Visual Interpretation of Artifacts in the Life Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21357)
*Elaine Lau,Thanuka Udumulla,Lee Izhaki-Tavor,Francisco Guzmán,Nicholas Magazine,Jonas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VIALS，一个包含161个视觉问答任务的生命科学领域基准，覆盖生物技术行业中实验工作流常见的科学图像（凝胶电泳、显微图像、质粒图谱、流式细胞图、分子结构等）。研究发现当前前沿视觉语言模型虽然能流畅描述自然图像，但在解读这些科学图像时表现不佳，暴露出领域知识和领域特定视觉推理能力的不足；而具备相关领域经验的科学家则能轻松完成这些任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生命科学专业工作流中，科学家普遍依赖对各类科学可视化图像（凝胶印迹、显微图像、质粒图谱、流式细胞图和分子结构等）的解读来做研究决策。然而现有视觉问答基准多基于出版物和教材中的精美图像，缺乏对实验工作流中实际产生的不完美图像进行解释的能力评估。这导致AI模型能否真正支撑专业生命科学工作流尚不明确。

Method: 作者构建了VIALS基准，包含161个视觉解释任务，覆盖生物技术行业实验工作流中检查的各种科学图像类型。基准中的任务由设计者基于实际实验场景精心设计，并通过专家验证。

Result: 实验结果显示，前沿视觉语言模型虽然能够流畅描述自然图像，但在准确解读这些科学图像方面表现不足，反映出当前模型在领域知识和领域特定视觉推理方面的局限性。作为对比，具有相关领域经验的科学家认为这些视觉解释任务相当直接。

Conclusion: 文章指出，缺乏解读此类科学图像能力的AI在专业生命科学工作流中的实用性将受限，因为这些图像是科学家推理、沟通和决策的核心载体。这凸显了开发具备领域知识增强和科学视觉推理能力的模型的重要性。

Abstract: In professional life sciences workflows, scientists routinely interpret visual artifacts (gel blots, microscopy images, plasmid maps, flow cytometry plots, molecular structures, ...) to inform research decisions. We introduce VIALS, a visual question-answering benchmark with 161 such interpretation tasks, spanning the types of artifacts examined throughout experimental workflows in the biotech industry (rather than polished figures from publications and textbooks). While frontier vision-language models can now fluently describe natural images, we find that they are unable to accurately interpret these scientific images, reflecting limitations in domain knowledge and domain-specific visual reasoning capabilities. In contrast, scientists with relevant domain expertise find these visual interpretation tasks straightforward. AI that cannot similarly interpret such images will have limited utility in professional life sciences workflows, where such artifacts are central to how scientists reason, communicate, and make decisions.

</details>

### [89] [RISE: Adaptive Imagination for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20430)
*Hongbo Lu,Liang Yao,Chenghao He,Hao Han,Fan Liu,Wenlong Liao,Tao He,Pai Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RISE，一个系统级的自适应想象框架，通过序列式Roll/Stop决策按需分配想象预算，并构造反事实数据集CounterDrive以丰富未来动态和提供风险监督，在NAVSIM和nuScenes上实现最优规划性能同时减少不必要的rollout计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World Action Models（WAMs）虽然通过引入未来世界演化提升了规划能力，但所有场景都分配固定的想象预算，缺乏对场景差异的感知，导致计算资源浪费或规划精度不足。

Method: 提出RISE框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）Latent Evaluator，评估当前前缀所揭示的风险以及继续rollout可能带来的规划提升；（2）Rollout Gate，权衡期望收益与额外计算成本，做出顺序的Roll/Stop决策。同时构建CounterDrive反事实数据集，利用反事实未来轨迹丰富动态多样性并提供局部风险监督，所有保留样本经专家验证和注释轨迹有效性、事件起始与因果类别。

Result: 在NAVSIM和nuScenes基准上的实验表明，RISE取得了最优的整体规划性能，同时减少了不必要的rollout；额外的迁移实验结果支持其作为插件在多种WAM架构中的通用性。

Conclusion: RISE证明了通过选择性rollout按需分配想象预算能够同时提升规划性能与计算效率，CounterDrive为安全关键的世界建模研究提供了可复用的反事实资源。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve planning by incorporating future world evolution into action generation, yet existing methods allocate a fixed imagination budget to every scene. We propose RISE (\textbf{R}efining \textbf{I}magination through \textbf{SE}lective Rollout), a system-level adaptive imagination framework that makes sequential \textsc{Roll}/\textsc{Stop} decisions according to the expected planning benefit of continued rollout. At each step, a Latent Evaluator estimates the risk revealed by the current prefix and how much planning could improve if imagination continues, while a Rollout Gate weighs this expected benefit against additional computation cost. Since factual driving logs expose only one realized future, we further construct \textbf{CounterDrive}, a counterfactual dataset with diverse outcomes and risk levels, to enrich future dynamics and provide localized risk supervision. Each retained sample undergoes expert verification and annotation of trajectory validity, incident onset, and causal category, providing a reusable resource for safety-critical world-modeling research. Experiments on NAVSIM and nuScenes show that RISE achieves the best overall planning performance while reducing unnecessary rollout, with additional transfer results supporting its plug-in generality across WAM architectures.

</details>


### [90] [Aggregating Visual Information with Optimal Transport for VideoLM Token Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20473)
*Wenti Yin,Xiaotian Han,Junyuan Shang,Yuchen Ding,Shuohuan Wang,Dianhai Yu,Changxin Gao,Nong Sang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVIOT提出使用最优传输将视频token压缩问题建模为从稠密帧观测经验测度到紧凑目标测度的输运问题，通过源到目标的耦合指定压缩表示构建方式，并结合任务（问题条件化）与空间（多粒度融合）两个维度的适配，在多种压缩比下匹配或超越未压缩基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型将视频编码为稠密的视觉token序列，存在大量表示冗余。压缩这些序列对降低语言模型解码时的视觉token负担至关重要，核心挑战在于压缩过程中保持分散在帧间的视觉信息不丢失。

Method: 将视频token压缩建模为最优传输问题：将稠密的帧观测经验测度传输到紧凑的目标测度，源到目标的耦合指定源观测在目标支持上的分布，从而直接定义压缩视频表示如何构建。此外沿两个轴适配：(1) 任务轴——问题条件化调制源帧与目标支持间的传输代价，同时影响分配给每个时间段的支撑数量，将表示容量导向与问题相关的内容；(2) 空间轴——在多个空间粒度上计算区域特定的时间传输计划并自适应融合其表示的输出，使同一紧凑表示内的不同区域可从不同时刻采样信息。

Result: 在多个视频理解基准上，不同压缩比下的评估表明AVIOT匹配或超越未压缩基线的性能，同时在更高压缩比下仍保持较强性能。

Conclusion: AVIOT通过最优传输框架实现了有效的视频token压缩，在保持或提升视频理解性能的同时显著降低视觉token负担，且压缩鲁棒性良好，在高压缩比下仍具竞争力。

Abstract: Video language models process videos as dense visual-token sequences with substantial representational redundancy. Compressing these sequences is therefore essential for reducing the visual-token burden on language-model decoding. The central challenge is to preserve visual information dispersed across frames under such compression. To this end, we introduce Aggregating Visual Information with Optimal Transport (AVIOT), which casts video token compression as transporting a dense empirical measure of frame observations onto a compact target measure. The resulting source-to-target coupling induces a distribution over source observations for each target support, directly specifying how the compressed video representation is constructed. We further adapt this construction along task and spatial axes. Question conditioning modulates the transport cost between source frames and target supports, while influencing how many supports are allocated to each temporal segment, thereby directing representation capacity toward question-relevant content. At multiple spatial granularities, AVIOT computes region-specific temporal transport plans and adaptively fuses the representations they yield, allowing different regions within the same compact representation to draw from different moments. Evaluations across varying compression ratios show that AVIOT matches or outperforms the uncompressed baseline on multiple video-understanding benchmarks while retaining strong performance at higher compression ratios.

</details>


### [91] [Annotations as Rollouts: Efficient and Scalable Reinforcement Learning for Video MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20492)
*Yunheng Li,Guohong Mu,Hao Li,Shengsheng Qian,Dingwen Zhang,Qibin Hou,Ming-Ming Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OraRL，一种用于视频多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的样本高效且可扩展的强化学习(RL)后训练方法。核心创新在于将标注数据不仅用于评分，还作为"oracle rollout"直接纳入on-policy组作为正向优化目标；并提出解耦优势估计器来解决直接集成oracle导致的优势反转(advantage inversion)问题；通过符号平衡剪枝提升效率。OraRL在模型规模从0.8B到9B上均超越基线，无需思维链即可在130ms内解码，在时间分割、跟踪、分割和空间智能等多个基准上显著超越先前最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频MLLMs的统一感知范式已逐渐成熟，然而在大型多任务数据集上进行后训练仍然具有挑战性。现有的RL方法在on-policy采样中仅产生少数高质量的rollouts，即使使用昂贵的思维链(CoT)生成也是如此。作者希望研究RL后训练在视频MLLM中的样本效率和可扩展性，并发现标注数据除了评分之外还有一个被忽视的作用——可以作为一种直接的正面优化目标（oracle rollout）进入on-policy组。

Method: OraRL的核心由两部分组成：(1) 解耦优势估计器(decoupled advantage estimator)：策略rollouts决定无oracle的基线，而oracle-策略差距同时调制一个方向性增益(directional gain)和一个独立的、被分离的oracle优势(detached oracle advantage)，从而避免因高奖励oracle抬高组基线而将策略优势翻转为负值的"优势反转"问题。(2) 符号平衡剪枝(sign-balanced pruning)：仅保留oracle和每个符号方向上的最强rollouts，从而极大提升计算效率。

Result: OraRL仅需SFT的2.2倍步时间，远少于GRPO+CoT所需的4.9倍。在0.8B到9B模型规模和最多10万提示词上都优于基线和GRPO。无需思维链时，Video-ORA-9B解码只需130ms而非4780ms。相比先前最优模型，时间mIoU从62.5提升至66.0，跟踪AO从73.0提升至78.2，分割从64.3提升至70.4，三基准空间智能宏平均从51.0提升至56.1；在VSI-Bench上得分73.1，而GPT-5为55.0、Gemini-3-Pro为55.1。

Conclusion: OraRL证明了标注数据作为oracle rollouts直接注入on-policy组的有效性，通过解耦优势估计器解决了优势反转问题，并借助符号平衡剪枝实现了显著的效率提升。该方法不仅在性能上大幅超越现有最强模型（包括闭源前沿模型），还展示了良好的可扩展性（随模型规模和数据量增长而提升），且无需昂贵的思维链生成即达到卓越的时空感知能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have become a prevailing paradigm for unified video perception. However, post-training on large multi-task datasets remains challenging, as existing reinforcement learning methods sample on-policy groups with few high-quality rollouts even with costly chain-of-thought (CoT) generation. In this paper, we study the sample efficiency and scalability of RL post-training for video MLLMs and introduce OraRL. We identify an overlooked role for annotations: Beyond scoring rollouts, each can enter its on-policy group as an oracle rollout, a direct positive optimization target. Direct oracle integration, however, is nontrivial: a high-reward oracle raises the group baseline and inverts otherwise positive policy advantages, a failure we term advantage inversion. At the core of OraRL is a decoupled advantage estimator: policy rollouts determine an oracle-free baseline, while the oracle-policy gap modulates both a directional gain and a separate detached oracle advantage. Sign-balanced pruning improves efficiency: by retaining only the oracle and the strongest rollouts of each sign, OraRL requires just 2.2x the step time of SFT, less than half the 4.9x required by GRPO with CoT. OraRL scales with model size and data, surpassing its backbone from 0.8B to 9B and GRPO up to 100k prompts. Without chain-of-thought, Video-ORA-9B decodes in 130 ms instead of 4,780 ms. Compared with the respective prior best models, it raises temporal mIoU from 62.5 to 66.0, tracking AO from 73.0 to 78.2, segmentation from 64.3 to 70.4, and the three-benchmark spatial-intelligence macro average from 51.0 to 56.1; on VSI-Bench, it scores 73.1 against 55.0 for GPT-5 and 55.1 for Gemini-3-Pro.

</details>


### [92] [DiffVC-ONE: Diffusion-based Generative Video Compression with One-Step Video Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20515)
*Wenzhuo Ma,Zhenzhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了DiffVC-ONE，一个基于单步视频扩散Transformer的生成式视频压缩框架，通过统一单向潜变量压缩器、单步扩散增强器和混合条件生成器，在低比特率下实现了卓越的感知质量和时间一致性，同时保持了较低的推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式视频压缩技术虽然能在低比特率下恢复丰富的视觉细节，但难以同时兼顾高时间一致性和低推理成本，这两者之间存在内在矛盾。

Method: DiffVC-ONE包含三个核心组件：1) 统一单向潜变量压缩器（使用共享模型高效均匀地压缩紧凑潜变量切片）；2) 基于视频DiT的单步扩散增强器（以重建潜变量切片为内容锚点，对整组图片执行单步时空感知增强）；3) 混合条件生成器（提取结构、强度和语义条件，分别用于保留忠实区域、控制生成增强程度和补充内容感知的感知细节）。

Result: 在多个标准基准上的大量实验表明，DiffVC-ONE在感知质量和时间一致性方面达到了最先进的水平，同时保持了较低的推理成本。

Conclusion: 通过单步扩散与多条件注入的设计，DiffVC-ONE有效地解决了生成式视频压缩中时间一致性、重建质量与推理效率之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Generative video compression can recover rich visual details at low bitrates, but simultaneously achieving high temporal consistency and low inference cost remains challenging. To address this issue, we propose DiffVC-ONE, a diffusion-based generative video compression framework built on a one-step Video Diffusion Transformer. First, we introduce a Unified Unidirectional Latent Compressor that uses a shared model to efficiently and uniformly compress compact latent slices. We then develop a Video DiT-based One-Step Diffusion Enhancer that uses the reconstructed latent slices as content anchors and performs single-step spatio-temporal perceptual enhancement over an entire group of pictures. Finally, a Hybrid Condition Generator extracts structural, strength, and semantic conditions from the reconstructed content and quantization information. These conditions preserve faithful regions, control the degree of generative enhancement, and supplement content-aware perceptual details during one-step diffusion enhancement. Extensive experiments on multiple standard benchmarks demonstrate that DiffVC-ONE achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality and temporal consistency with low inference cost.

</details>


### [93] [Grounded-Exo2Ego: Structured Semantic Grounding for Robust Exocentric-to-Egocentric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20534)
*Shengze Wang,Michael Stengel,Tianye Li,Seonwook Park,Amrita Mazumdar,Koki Nagano,Alex Trevithick,Shalini De Mello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Grounded-Exo2Ego框架，用于从单个外视角视频生成自我中心（第一人称）视频。该框架通过双分支视频扩散模型（几何锚定分支+语义接地分支）以及相机重定位算法和自动化合成数据引擎，在EgoExo4D数据集上大幅超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外视角到自我中心视角的视频生成是AR/VR和物理AI领域的新兴重要课题。与传统的视图合成相比，该任务因极端视角变化和大量不可观测区域导致标准几何条件化极不可靠，因此更具挑战性。

Method: 提出了Grounded-Exo2Ego框架：(1) 架构层面采用双分支视频扩散模型，包含依赖3D重建渲染的几何锚定分支，以及基于物体级上下文合成困难区域的新型语义接地分支；(2) 引入相机重定位算法解决相机与重建不对齐的问题；(3) 开发全自动合成数据引擎，在程序化生成的环境中生成并渲染绑定的3D角色。

Result: 在具有挑战性的EgoExo4D数据集上，该方法在所有指标上大幅优于近期最先进的方法。详细的消融实验验证了数据层面和架构层面各贡献的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过架构和数据的双重改进，Grounded-Exo2Ego有效解决了外视角到自我中心视频生成中几何条件不可靠和相机重建不对齐的问题，为该领域提供了突破性的解决方案。

Abstract: Generating egocentric video from a single exocentric video is an emerging and important topic for AR/VR and physical AI. Compared with conventional novel view synthesis, exo-to-ego generation is a significantly harder task because the standard geometric conditioning becomes highly unreliable under extreme view changes and large unobservable regions. We present Grounded-Exo2Ego, a principled framework that addresses these challenges at both the architectural and data levels. Architecturally, Grounded-Exo2Ego is a dual-branch video diffusion model that couples a geometric anchoring branch, which conditions the generation on the rendering of a 3D reconstruction, with a novel semantic grounding branch, which goes beyond the prevailing geometry-based approach and improves quality by synthesizing challenging regions based on object-level context. Additionally, we found that the overlooked issue of camera-reconstruction misalignment severely undermines exo-to-ego learning. We thus introduce a camera re-localization algorithm that resolves this issue and substantially improves quality across all metrics. We further develop a fully automated synthetic data engine that generates and renders rigged 3D characters in procedurally generated environments. Evaluation on the challenging EgoExo4D dataset shows that our method outperforms recent state-of-the-art approaches by large margins across all metrics. Detailed ablations validate improvements from each of our contributions at both the data and architectural level.

</details>


### [94] [Keep Your Friends Close, and the Right Neighbours Closer: Disaster-Conditioned Kernel-Regularized Graph Attention for Building Damage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20548)
*Fuad Hasan,Chul Min Yeum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究灾害损坏分类中的空间上下文建模问题，提出一种基于灾害类型条件的图模型，通过可学习多尺度空间核先验注入注意力机制，并加入残差去相关损失，在xBD数据集上通过留一事件验证和跨数据集迁移验证，显著提升宏F1并降低残差空间自相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害损坏本质上是空间性的，建筑很少孤立失效，但现有分类管线仍主要依赖单建筑外观线索，忽视了空间结构带来的主导不确定性。且不同灾害（洪水、飓风、野火）的空间聚类行为差异大，朴素上下文聚合虽能提升视觉一致性但会过平滑边界或传播结构化错误，需要一种能自适应灾害类型空间特性的建模方法。

Method: 采用受控的后定位-仅分类设置：每个建筑用预/后组合（PPC）补丁表示，空间上下文用GPS导出的建筑图建模。核心方法包括：(1) 灾害类型条件图模型，将可学习的多尺度空间核先验注入注意力机制，使有效邻域尺度能随灾害类型自适应；(2) 残差去相关损失，惩罚预测残差中的正Moran's I，抑制"通过平滑获得一致性"的倾向。

Result: 在xBD数据集上采用留一事件验证（LOEO）协议评估事件及数据集偏移，并跨数据集从xBD迁移到Ida-BD。模型在零样本事件偏移下改善了宏F1，并大幅降低了残差空间自相关，表明更好地利用了空间上下文而非朴素平滑，能更可靠地迁移到已知灾害类型内的未见事件。

Conclusion: 通过灾害类型条件化的图注意力机制和残差去相关约束，可以在利用空间上下文提升灾害损坏分类性能的同时避免过平滑问题，实现更强的零样本事件泛化与跨数据集迁移能力。

Abstract: Disaster damage is spatial: buildings rarely fail in isolation. Yet using spatial context for damage classification remains surprisingly underexplored, and many pipelines still rely primarily on per-building appearance cues even when the dominant uncertainty is spatially structured. Complicating matters, the right neighbourhood is not the same across events. Floods, hurricanes, and wildfires can exhibit very different clustering behaviour, making spatial reasoning valuable but easy to misuse - naive context aggregation can improve visual coherence while oversmoothing boundaries or propagating structured errors. We study this tension on xBD (the dataset used in the xView2 challenge) in a controlled post-localization, classification-only setup: each building is represented by a pre/post combined (PPC) patch cropped from the provided polygons, and spatial context is modelled with GPS-derived building graphs. Our approach keeps local evidence "close" by preserving strong spatial relationships in disaster damage patterns, while bringing only the right neighbours "closer" through a disaster-type-conditioned graph model that injects a learnable multi-scale spatial kernel prior into attention, allowing the effective neighbourhood scale to adapt across disaster types rather than being learned as a single global smoothing rule. To discourage coherence-by-smoothing, we add a residual de-correlation loss that penalizes positive Moran's~I in prediction residuals. We evaluate the method under event and dataset shift with a leave-one-event-out (LOEO) protocol on xBD and cross-dataset transfer from xBD to Ida-BD. The model improves macro-F1 and substantially reduces residual spatial autocorrelation under zero-shot event shift, indicating better use of spatial context rather than naive smoothing and enabling more reliable transfer to unseen events within known disaster types.

</details>


### [95] [Learning Prostate Anatomy at Test Time for Cancer Detection in Micro-Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20557)
*Obed Korshie Dzikunu,Mohammad Mahdi Abootorabi,Mohamed Harmanani,Paul F. R. Wilson,Emma Willis,Ferdinand Luger,Adam Kinnaird,Brian Wodlinger,Parvin Mousavi,Purang Abolmaesumi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ANT是一个分割引导的测试时自适应（TTA）框架，通过冻结的分割网络生成的伪掩膜来指导预训练的前列腺癌检测编码器在测试时解决辅助前列腺分割任务，从而将编码器表征与目标域的解剖结构对齐，纠正域特异性特征漂移同时保留癌症判别结构。在跨中心跨代微超声数据上，ANT在活检核心和患者级别分别比无自适应提升2.9%和3.6%的平均AUC，优于现有TTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同临床中心因成像硬件或采集协议不同导致的域偏移是深度学习模型用于前列腺癌检测部署的根本障碍。现有TTA方法（如熵最小化或基于增强的自监督）只纠正图像外观上的统计差异，但忽略了目标域的解剖结构，导致特征漂移未能充分校正。

Method: 提出ANT框架，一个分割引导的TTA方法：在测试时，利用一个冻结的预训练分割网络生成伪掩膜作为监督信号，让预训练的癌症检测编码器通过求解辅助前列腺分割任务来适应目标域。该分割任务将编码器表征与目标域的前列腺解剖结构对齐，纠正域特异性特征漂移，同时保留癌症判别性结构。

Result: 模型在693例患者（早期代微超声扫描仪、多中心临床试验）上训练，在118例患者（新一代系统、两个中心的另一项临床试验）上评估。在留一中心法（leave-one-center-out）协议且所有方法评估条件相同的设置下，ANT相比无自适应在活检核心和患者级别平均AUC分别提升2.9%和3.6%，优于TTA基线方法。

Conclusion: ANT通过将解剖结构信息融入测试时自适应过程，有效解决了跨中心跨代成像系统带来的域偏移问题，显著提升了前列腺癌检测的泛化性能。相较仅关注统计外观差异的现有TTA方法，引入目标域解剖结构约束是提高域适应效果的关键。代码已开源。

Abstract: Domain shift across clinical centers using different imaging hardware or acquisition protocols remains a fundamental barrier to deploying deep learning models for prostate cancer (PCa) detection. Existing test-time adaptation (TTA) methods address distribution shift through entropy minimization or augmentation-based self-supervision, correcting for statistical differences in image appearance but ignoring the anatomical structure of the target domain. We propose ANT, a segmentation-guided TTA framework that adapts a pretrained cancer detection encoder to the target domain by solving an auxiliary prostate segmentation task at test time, supervised by pseudo-masks from a frozen pretrained segmentation network. By aligning encoder representations to prostate anatomy in the target domain, ANT corrects domain-specific feature drift while preserving cancer-discriminative structure. The model was trained on 693 patients imaged with an earlier-generation micro-ultrasound scanner in a multi-center clinical trial, and evaluated on 118 patients acquired with a newer-generation system across two centers in another clinical trial. Under a leave-one-center-out protocol with identical evaluation conditions across all methods, ANT improves mean AUC by 2.9% and 3.6% at the biopsy-core and patient levels, respectively, over no adaptation, outperforming TTA baselines. Code is available at: https://github.com/ObedDzik/ant.git.

</details>


### [96] [Zero-Shot Color Image Manipulation Localization via Noise Residual Artifact Pattern Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20558)
*Edgar Gonzalez-Fernandez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种零样本、无需训练的无源图像伪造定位流水线，直接从单张可疑图像的噪声残差中估计参考伪影模式，无需预设滤镜配置、颜色布局或块周期，通过去噪器选择准则、块级相关分析和双分量高斯混合模型评分生成像素级篡改概率图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的被动图像伪造检测方法主要依赖拜耳残差的绿色通道，丢弃了其余颜色通道中的相关信息，且通常需要训练数据或设备注册，无法满足实际场景中零样本、即时的篡改定位需求。

Method: 提出三条核心组件：(1) 基于采集噪声与插值噪声方差比原则的去噪器选择准则；(2) 针对估计参考模式进行块级相关性分析；(3) 双分量高斯混合模型评分阶段，生成像素级篡改概率图。方法不假设固定的滤波器配置、颜色布局或块周期。

Result: 通过消融实验评估了去噪器选择与块大小对定位精度的影响，并与现有最先进被动方法对比，证明了该零样本方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法在无需训练数据或设备先验信息的情况下即可实现竞争性的图像篡改定位性能，为零样本盲取证提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Digital cameras embed device-specific artifacts into every acquired image through demosaicing, in-camera post-processing, and lossy compression. These traces constitute a forensic signal that can be exploited to assess image authenticity. Existing passive methods rely predominantly on the green channel of the Bayer residual, discarding the correlated information available in the remaining color channels and typically requiring training data or device enrollment. This work proposes a zero-shot, training-free blind image manipulation localization pipeline that estimates a reference artifact pattern directly from the noise residual of a single suspect image, without assuming a fixed filter configuration, color layout, or block period. The pipeline incorporates a principled denoiser selection criterion based on the acquired-to-interpolated noise variance ratio, a block-level correlation analysis against the estimated reference pattern, and a two-component Gaussian Mixture Model scoring stage that produces a pixel-level tampering probability map. An ablation study evaluates the impact of denoiser choice and block size on localization accuracy, and comparisons against state-of-the-art passive methods demonstrate the competitiveness of the proposed zero-shot approach.

</details>


### [97] [Aggregate, Don't Adapt: Subject-Level Posterior Aggregation and Transductive Calibration for Cross-Site Parkinsonian Gait Severity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20587)
*Junlong Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MoCha 2026帕金森步态基准与挑战赛的获胜方案，该系统在隐藏测试集上达到0.6945 macro-F1，在58个参赛队伍中排名第一，大幅领先第二名（0.5807）和官方基线（0.4289）。其核心优势并非来自模型创新，而是三个通常被视为“记账”环节的处理步骤：精确复现基准的头部结构、按组织方提供的受试者分组对逐走步后验进行平均、以及无需标签的转导式特征均值与决策操作点校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在揭示在临床站点未见过的训练数据上预测MDS-UPDRS步态严重程度的挑战中，常被忽视的工程细节（如数据处理与后处理）可以比模型架构本身带来更大的性能提升。同时，作者也指出了自建交叉验证方法与实际决策分数之间的反相关现象，希望为社区提供全面的负面实验记录。

Method: 系统使用冻结的公共运动编码器，仅附加一个4×512的线性层。方法包含三个关键阶段：(1) 精确复现参考基准的头部配方；(2) 在组织方提供的受试者分组内对逐走步后验概率进行平均聚合；(3) 无需标签的转导式校准（同时调整特征均值与决策操作点）。作者还进行了消融实验，包括四种微调编码器方案和十种备选编码器对比。

Result: 在隐藏测试集上达到0.6945 macro-F1，排名58个参赛队伍中的第一，超过第二名（0.5807）约0.11，超过官方基线（0.4289）约0.27。性能增益几乎全部来自三个被轻视的处理阶段。而微调编码器在四种形式下均导致性能下降，十种备选编码器也均不如冻结方案。此外，作者的自建leave-two-cohort-out交叉验证在11个配置上与最终评分呈反相关。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：在此基准上，最大增益来源是受试者级别的聚合，这构成了该基准的成绩天花板。同时，研究者应警惕交叉验证设计不当可能带来的误导性结论，低频工程细节（head复现、后处理聚合、转导校准）在临床步态类任务中可能比模型架构探索更具决定性作用。

Abstract: We describe the winning entry to the MoCha 2026 Benchmark and Challenge on Parkinsonian Gait, which predicts MDS-UPDRS gait severity from canonicalized SMPL motion recorded at clinical sites unseen during training. The system reaches 0.6945 macro-F1 on the hidden test and ranked first of 58 entries, ahead of the runner-up at 0.5807 and the organizers' baseline at 0.4289, on a frozen public motion encoder with a single $4\times512$ linear layer. Nearly all of the margin comes from three stages usually treated as bookkeeping: reproducing the reference benchmark's exact head recipe, averaging per-walk posteriors within the subject grouping the organizers ship, and a label-free transductive calibration of the feature mean and the decision operating point. Fine-tuning the encoder lost in four distinct forms, and ten alternative encoders were worse. Every ablation number is a paid read on the hidden test, because our own leave-two-cohort-out cross-validation proved anti-correlated with the deciding score over eleven configurations. We give the negative record in full, and identify our largest gain, subject-level aggregation, as the binding ceiling on this benchmark.

</details>


### [98] [A Dataset-Centric Benchmark of Deep Learning Methods for Grape Leaf Disease Classification and Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20608)
*Petar Canoski,Vlatko Spasev,Ivica Dimitrovski,Ivan Kitanovski,Petre Lameski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以数据集为中心的深度学习基准，用于葡萄叶病害分类与检测。作者系统分析了公开数据集在病害类别、标注类型、采集条件、图像特征、类别分布、来源和任务适用性等方面的差异，并在图像级分类、区域级分类和目标检测三种设置下评估了代表性模型，同时进行了跨数据集迁移实验。结果显示受控条件下的分类性能接近饱和，异构数据集难度更大，检测性能随标注设置大幅波动，跨数据集性能显著下降。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有葡萄叶病害识别研究大多依赖在受控条件下采集的少量数据集，难以反映真实葡萄园中的复杂背景、光照变化、遮挡、叶片姿态、病害严重程度和采集设备差异等实际挑战，导致模型在现实场景中的泛化能力缺乏可信评估。

Method: 论文采用数据集中心（dataset-centric）的基准构建方法：首先对公开数据集进行多维度分析（病害类别、标注类型、采集条件、图像特征、类别分布、来源和任务适用性）；然后分别在图像级分类（用accuracy评估）、区域级分类和目标检测（用mAP@50和mAP@50:95评估）三种设置下评估代表性深度学习模型；最后进行跨数据集实验，考察在病害类别兼容但视觉和标注特征不同的数据集之间的迁移性能。

Result: 在多个受控或衍生数据集上分类性能接近饱和；在异构数据集上难度明显增大；检测性能在不同标注设置下存在显著差异；跨数据集的性能大幅下降，尤其在目标检测任务上，表明共享病害标签并不等同于定义等价识别任务。

Conclusion: 可靠的葡萄园病害识别需要对数据集来源进行严格审查、在真实田间条件下进行验证、确保标注兼容性，并进行外部验证，而非仅仅依赖受控条件下的高分类准确率。

Abstract: Grape leaf disease recognition is important for precision agriculture, enabling early diagnosis, timely intervention, and improved vineyard management. Although deep learning has achieved strong results, many studies rely on few datasets, often acquired under controlled conditions, and may not reflect real vineyard challenges such as complex backgrounds, variable illumination, occlusion, leaf pose, disease severity, and device differences. This paper presents a dataset-centric benchmark of deep learning methods for grape leaf disease classification and detection. We analyze publicly available datasets in terms of disease categories, annotation types, acquisition conditions, image characteristics, class distributions, provenance, and task suitability. Representative models are evaluated in three settings: image-level classification, region-level classification, and object detection. Classification is assessed using accuracy, while detection is evaluated using mAP@50 and mAP@50:95. Cross-dataset experiments further examine transfer between datasets with compatible disease categories but different visual and annotation characteristics. Results show near-saturated classification performance on several controlled or derivative datasets, greater difficulty on heterogeneous datasets, and substantial variation in detection performance across annotation settings. Cross-dataset performance drops sharply, especially for object detection, indicating that shared disease labels do not necessarily define equivalent recognition tasks. The benchmark emphasizes dataset provenance, realistic field evaluation, annotation compatibility, and external validation for reliable vineyard disease recognition.

</details>


### [99] [RECOUNT: Reference-guided Counting with Synthetic Visual Exemplars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20621)
*Adriano D'Alessandro,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri,Ghassan Hamarneh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RECOUNT是一个即插即用的图像引导零样本计数框架，通过将文本提示替换为单个场景外参考图像，并利用扩散模型将其扩展为多样化示例库，在冻结计数器上实现了最优的零样本计数精度，将计数误差分别降低55%（LookAlikes）和21%（PairTally）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导的零样本计数方法在空间定位上表现出色，但在新颖或细粒度类别上分类性能较差，因为自然语言过于粗糙，难以完整刻画视觉身份，无法区分视觉上相似的干扰物。而少样本计数虽使用视觉示例解决了这一问题，却要求对每张图像进行人工标注。为化解这一两难困境，论文提出用视觉方式而非文本方式指定类别。

Method: RECOUNT的核心思路是：1) 用户提供单个场景外参考图像来指定目标类别，而非文本提示；2) 发现单一参考图像对类别外观覆盖过窄且跨场景不可靠，因此将扩散模型改造为自动化对比数据引擎，将参考图像扩展为多样化的示例图库；3) 保留冻结计数器的类无关候选框生成能力，将分类任务外包给独立的视觉模块（冻结骨干网络加轻量级分类头，在合成数据上训练），将每个候选框与目标和干扰物图库进行匹配。

Result: 将RECOUNT应用于冻结计数器后，在两个基准上均取得了最优的零样本计数精度，在LookAlikes上将计数误差（MAE）相比最强先前零样本计数器降低了55%，在PairTally上降低了21%。

Conclusion: RECOUNT成功解决了文本引导与少样本方法之间的两难困境，证明通过图像引导与扩散模型数据增强，可以在无需人工标注的情况下实现对细粒度类别的精确零样本计数。

Abstract: Text-guided zero-shot object counters excel at spatial localization but categorize poorly on novel or fine-grained classes: natural language is too coarse to fully specify visual identity, so they fail to separate visually similar distractors. Few-shot counters sidestep this with visual exemplars, but require manual annotations on every image. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce RECOUNT, a plug-and-play framework for image-guided zero-shot counting. Rather than specify a category with a text prompt, our key insight is to specify it visually, from a single off-scene reference image. However, we find that a lone reference image provides narrow coverage of a category's appearance and is unreliable across diverse scenes. We therefore repurpose a diffusion model as an automated contrastive data engine that expands the reference into a diverse exemplar gallery, supplying the discriminative detail that text cannot. RECOUNT preserves the class-agnostic proposals of any frozen counter and offloads categorization to a separate visual module (a frozen backbone with a lightweight head trained on this synthetic data) that matches each proposal against the target and distractor galleries. Applied to a frozen counter, RECOUNT attains the best zero-shot accuracy on both benchmarks, cutting counting error (MAE) by 55% on LookAlikes and 21% on PairTally relative to the strongest prior zero-shot counter.

</details>


### [100] [MV2GF: Multi-view Pedestrian Detection with a Visual Geometric Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20639)
*Taiga Yamane,Satoshi Suzuki,Ryo Masumura,Shota Orihashi,Tomohiro Tanaka,Mana Ihori,Naoki Makishima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MV2GF，利用视觉几何基础模型解决多视角行人检测（MVPD）在未见过的相机配置下的泛化问题。通过融合任务特定特征与基础模型提取的通用几何特征，并利用基础模型预测的3D点图将图像特征投影到合适的3D位置，MV2GF在未见相机配置下比现有方法具有更好的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MVPD方法采用统一的框架将2D图像特征投影到3D世界空间并聚合成单一特征，虽然有效，但在训练中未见过的相机配置下难以泛化。主要原因有二：一是难以在未知相机配置下捕获跨视图的准确视觉几何；二是检测模型高度依赖于训练时图像特征投影产生的畸变模式。

Method: MV2GF利用一个视觉几何基础模型，该模型在捕获跨视图视觉几何和预测不同相机配置下的3D属性方面表现出强泛化能力。具体包括两个关键设计：(1) 将任务特定特征与基础模型提取的通用几何特征融合，以有效捕获未知相机配置下的视觉几何；(2) 利用基础模型预测的3D点图将图像特征中的每个像素投影到合适的3D位置，从而避免检测模型依赖于训练时的畸变模式。

Result: 实验证明，利用视觉几何基础模型进行MVPD是有效的，MV2GF在泛化性能上优于现有方法，特别是在训练中未见过的相机配置下表现更佳。

Conclusion: 视觉几何基础模型能够有效提升多视角行人检测在未见过的相机配置下的泛化能力，MV2GF通过融合几何特征和基于3D点图的特征投影，成功解决了视觉几何捕获困难和畸变模式依赖两大问题。

Abstract: Multi-View Pedestrian Detection (MVPD) aims to detect pedestrians in the form of a bird's eye view map from multi-view images. Recent MVPD methods adopt a unified framework that projects 2D image features into a 3D world space and aggregates them into a single feature. Although they are effective, they struggle to generalize to unseen camera configurations during training due to two main issues. First, they are difficult to capture accurate visual geometry across views in unseen camera configurations. Second, they make detection models highly dependent on distortion patterns during training arising from their image feature projection. To address these, we leverage a visual geometric foundation model and propose MV2GF. This foundation model has exhibited strong generalization in capturing visual geometry across views and predicting accurate 3D attributes in diverse camera configurations. MV2GF fuses task-specific features with general-purpose geometric features extracted by the foundation model to effectively capture the visual geometry even in unseen camera configurations. Furthermore, MV2GF projects each pixel in the image features to an appropriate 3D location using 3D pointmaps predicted by the foundation model, preventing the detection model from depending on distortion patterns during training. Our experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of leveraging a visual geometric foundation model for MVPD and that MV2GF generalizes better than existing methods.

</details>


### [101] [Lift, Associate, and Fuse: A Decision-Centric Framework for 2D-to-3D Foundation Model Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20659)
*Wentao Sun,Yiping Chen,John S. Zelek,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LAF（Lift, Associate, and Fuse）决策中心框架，将2D基础模型预测迁移到3D分割的传输系统表示为五个算子：Generate、Associate、Reconcile、Fuse和Persist/Query。该框架定义了持久载体的显式契约，并通过对161个系统（截至2026年8月7日）的结构化审计协议，揭示了四个反复出现的性质，为比较、诊断和设计可修订的3D感知系统提供了表示中立的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的方法分组方式（按任务或按表示）掩盖了决定系统跨视角一致性的关键决策，包括：图像证据在哪里落地、观察何时成为同一身份、语义和粒度冲突如何处理、哪些信息被融合、哪些状态在后继查询中保留。需要一个决策中心框架来揭示这些被掩盖的结构性问题。

Method: 将传输系统形式化为五个算子（Generate, Associate, Reconcile, Fuse, Persist/Query），定义持久载体的显式契约（空间支持、语义状态、身份状态、不确定性、来源、支持的操作），识别证据被丢弃后不可恢复的第一阶段。将该框架操作化为结构化审计协议，应用于161个系统，涵盖点、场、高斯、对象、图、基于记忆的载体，并执行表示、时间、关系和前馈压力测试。

Result: 决策追踪暴露四个反复性质：1) 关联不建立身份；2) 载体设计同时决定查询接口和修正边界；3) 渲染视图、原生3D和提案级评估不可互换；4) 修饰语（如training-free、real-time、open-vocabulary、generalizable）只有附着在特定阶段并附完整成本账本时才有意义。

Conclusion: LAF提供了一种表示中立的框架，用于比较现有系统、诊断不可逆故障，并为未来智能体指定可修订的3D感知系统，从而改变对2D到3D传输系统的理解、评估和构建方式。

Abstract: Methods that transfer predictions from two-dimensional foundation models into three-dimensional segmentation are commonly grouped by task or representation. Those groupings obscure the decisions that determine whether a system remains coherent across views: where image evidence is grounded, when observations become one identity, how semantic and granularity conflicts are handled, which information is fused, and what state survives for later queries. We introduce \textbf{Lift, Associate, and Fuse (LAF)}, a decision-centric framework that represents a transfer system as five operators: \textbf{Generate, Associate, Reconcile, Fuse, and Persist/Query}. LAF defines an explicit contract for the persistent carrier---its spatial support, semantic state, identity state, uncertainty, provenance, and supported operations---and identifies the first stage at which discarded evidence becomes unrecoverable. We operationalize the framework as a structured audit protocol and apply it to 161 systems available through 7 August 2026, spanning point-, field-, Gaussian-, object-, graph-, and memory-based carriers. Representation, temporal, relational, and feed-forward stress tests required no additional analytical stage after the final confirmation pass. The resulting decision traces expose four recurring properties: association does not establish identity; carrier design fixes both the query interface and correction boundary; rendered-view, native-3D, and proposal-level evaluations are not interchangeable; and qualifiers such as \emph{training-free}, \emph{real-time}, \emph{open-vocabulary}, and \emph{generalizable} are meaningful only when attached to a stage and a complete cost ledger. LAF therefore supplies a representation-neutral method for comparing existing systems, diagnosing irreversible failures, and specifying revisable 3D perception for future agents.

</details>


### [102] [Shortcut Learning in a Public Grape Disease Dataset: Annotation Granularity as a Modulator, Not a Cause](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20663)
*Pushuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示农业病害检测数据集中标注粒度不一致是性能指标不可靠的根源：一类以整叶级别标注而另五类以病斑级别标注，导致模型在跨物种图像上产生严重虚假阳性（集中于该整叶类，过表达13.41倍）。反向反事实实验证明标注粒度只是放大/衰减捷径的调节器而非成因。文中还提供了无需图像和训练的粒度筛查统计量，并证明机载病斑级检测在光学上不可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅凭报告指标判断公共农业病害数据集是否适用，但这些指标无法反映标注方案内部是否一致。作者发现模型容量、输入分辨率和检测范式的变化带来的mAP50波动与随机种子噪声相当，瓶颈在于小物体检测，进而追查发现数据集标注粒度不一致这一被忽略的数据侧问题。

Method: 在3288张图像的葡萄病害数据集上，使用5种架构改变模型容量/分辨率/检测范式进行测试；随后在5156张无葡萄的跨物种图像上评估虚假阳性；通过反事实重训练（仅缩小该类别的框）验证因果效应，并以预先登记标准的反向操作（细化最粗类别至整叶级别）作为安慰剂对照。另外提出不需图像与训练的粒度筛查统计量。

Result: 跨物种图像中65.7%的虚假阳性落入唯一整叶级标注的类别（相对训练标注占比过表达13.41x）；仅缩小该类别的框可减少66%虚假阳性，安慰剂对照确认效应针对操作类别；反向操作（细化最细类别）虽提高了分布内AP，却产生零虚假阳性，证明粒度仅调节而非创造捷径。

Conclusion: 标注粒度不一致是放大虚假阳性捷径的调节器而非成因——它可放大或衰减已存在的汇聚点，却无法创造新汇聚点。该失效模式对分布内评估完全不可见，正确标注方式的标准仍未解决。机载病斑级检测被证明在光学上不可达。

Abstract: Public datasets for agricultural disease detection are usually judged fit for use from reported metrics, which say nothing about whether the annotation scheme is internally consistent. On one public grape disease dataset (3288 images, 11995 boxes, 6 classes), varying model capacity, input resolution and detection paradigm yields a test-set mAP50 range comparable to seed-to-seed noise, with the bottleneck at small objects across all five architectures. The finding lies on the data side: one class is annotated at whole-leaf level (median box area 43.16% of the image) while the other five are annotated at lesion level. On 5156 cross-species images containing no grape, 65.7% of the false-positive boxes fall into that one class, an over-representation of 13.41x relative to its share of the training annotations. Counterfactual retraining establishes a causal effect of granularity on the magnitude of the shortcut: shrinking only that class's boxes cuts its cross-species false positives by 66%, and a placebo control confirms the effect is specific to the manipulated class. A manipulation in the opposite direction, with criteria registered in advance, returns a negative result: coarsening the finest class to whole-leaf level (0.57% to 40.37%), matched in box count and share of annotations and with higher in-distribution AP, still leaves its cross-species false positives at zero boxes, while the unmanipulated original class holds 50.0% of them. Annotation granularity is therefore a modulator of this shortcut, not its cause: it can amplify or attenuate a sink that already exists, but cannot create one, and what fixes the destination remains open. We also give a granularity screening statistic requiring neither images nor training, and show airborne lesion-level detection to be optically out of reach. The failure mode is invisible to in-distribution evaluation.

</details>


### [103] [Aristotelian Manifolds: Leveraging Platonic Perceptual Features for Backpropagation Free Rapid Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20682)
*Michael Karnes,Alper Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文形式化并系统刻画了"亚里士多德流形"（Aristotelian Manifolds），这是一个基于柏拉图表征假设（Platonic Representation Hypothesis）构建的广义结构框架。作者将高容量基础模型定位为通用感知过滤器，并进行了逐层（layer-wise）综合研究，以映射知识在潜在子空间中的功能合成方式。在多种架构范式和跨域数据集上，作者系统刻画了网络深度、维度约简与距离度量之间的相互作用。研究揭示语义成熟并非遵循单一单调路径，不同数据域呈现高度差异化的几何响应特征：专业临床模态表现为中间丘状峰值，自然视觉任务则呈现S形平台。通过定位表征效率峰值的精确坐标，作者建立了可预测的层选择与特征压缩分类体系，最终证明对冻结表征的内部几何进行映射，能够提供一个无需反向传播、可解释且健壮的框架来理解和利用基础模型的潜在空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于基础模型潜在空间的研究通常假设语义表征随网络深度呈现单调演化的趋势，且缺乏跨架构、跨域的统一分析框架。本文旨在基于柏拉图表征假设，形式化一种广义结构框架（亚里士多德流形），从而系统刻画不同数据域在深层网络中的几何演化规律，建立可预测的层选择与特征压缩方法。

Method: 本文提出"亚里士多德流形"作为形式化框架，将基础模型视为通用感知过滤器。研究采用逐层分析方法，在多种架构范式和多域数据集上，系统考察网络深度、维度约简策略与距离度量之间的耦合关系，并通过刻画表征效率峰值的精确坐标建立层选择分类体系，全程基于冻结表征（无需反向传播）。

Result: 研究发现语义成熟并非单调过程：专业临床模态呈现中间丘状峰值（mound-like peaks），而自然视觉任务呈现S形平台（sigmoidal plateaus），表明不同数据域具有截然不同的几何响应轮廓。基于这些特征，作者构建了可预测的层选择与特征压缩分类体系，并验证了基于几何映射的冻结表征分析框架的有效性、健壮性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 本文证明，通过系统映射冻结表征的内部几何结构，可以在无需反向传播的前提下，为理解和利用基础模型的潜在空间提供一个健壮且可解释的统一框架。这一"亚里士多德流形"框架不仅揭示了不同数据域的差异化语义成熟路径，还为层选择与特征压缩提供了可操作的预测性指导。

Abstract: This paper formalizes and systematically characterizes Aristotelian Manifolds, a generalized structural framework built upon the Platonic Representation Hypothesis. We position high-capacity foundation models as universal perceptual filters and conduct a comprehensive layer-wise investigation to map how knowledge is functionally synthesized within these latent subspaces. Across diverse architectural paradigms and multi-domain datasets, we rigorously chart the interplay between network depth, dimensionality reduction, and distance metrics. Our characterization reveals that semantic maturation does not follow a singular, monotonic path; instead, different data domains exhibit highly distinct geometric response profiles, characterized by intermediate mound-like peaks for specialized clinical modalities and sigmoidal plateaus for natural visual tasks. By profiling the exact coordinates where these manifolds achieve peak representational efficiency, we establish a predictable taxonomy for layer selection and feature compression.
